# Guanzhi 官制 - 封王/封公授予效果. root=君主, scope:recipient=受封者
# (头衔创建必须在君主作用域: root=受封者时 change_title_holder 实测不生效)

# 册封齐王
guanzhi_wj_grant_wang_0000 = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = kingdom
		name = gzw_title_wang_0000
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0000
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0000
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0009
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

# 册封卫王
guanzhi_wj_grant_wang_0010 = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = kingdom
		name = gzw_title_wang_0010
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0010
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0010
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动

			limit = { is_landed = no }

		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		set_coa = gzw_coa_0019
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0019
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0029
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0029
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = kingdom
		name = gzw_title_wang_0039
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0039
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0039
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

# 册封河东王
guanzhi_wj_grant_wang_0049 = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = kingdom
		name = gzw_title_wang_0049
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0049
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0049
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0058
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0068
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0068
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = kingdom
		name = gzw_title_wang_0078
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0078
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0078
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

# 册封零陵王
guanzhi_wj_grant_wang_0088 = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = kingdom
		name = gzw_title_wang_0088
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0088
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0088
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0097
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0107
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0107
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = kingdom
		name = gzw_title_wang_0117
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0117
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0117
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

# 册封广阳王
guanzhi_wj_grant_wang_0127 = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = kingdom
		name = gzw_title_wang_0127
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0127
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0127
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_wang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_wang_0136
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_wang
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_wang
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0003
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0003
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = duchy
		name = gzw_title_gong_0013
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0013
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0013
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

# 册封虞公
guanzhi_wj_grant_gong_0023 = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = duchy
		name = gzw_title_gong_0023
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0023
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0023
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0032
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0042
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0042
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

# 册封汝南公
guanzhi_wj_grant_gong_0052 = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = duchy
		name = gzw_title_gong_0052
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0052
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0052
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

# 册封河间公
guanzhi_wj_grant_gong_0062 = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = duchy
		name = gzw_title_gong_0062
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0062
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0062
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动

			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0071
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0071
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

# 册封乐安公
guanzhi_wj_grant_gong_0081 = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = duchy
		name = gzw_title_gong_0081
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0081
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0081
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

# 册封长沙公
guanzhi_wj_grant_gong_0091 = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = duchy
		name = gzw_title_gong_0091
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0091
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0091
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0100
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0100
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

# 册封金城公
guanzhi_wj_grant_gong_0110 = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = duchy
		name = gzw_title_gong_0110
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0110
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0110
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

# 册封西河公
guanzhi_wj_grant_gong_0120 = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = duchy
		name = gzw_title_gong_0120
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0120
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0120
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0129
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0129
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}

# 册封合浦公
guanzhi_wj_grant_gong_0139 = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		# 王公爵与列侯/唐爵不共存: 册封时先削除侯爵; 同池授新替旧
		guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = yes
		guanzhi_clear_wangjue_effect = yes
	}
	create_dynamic_title = {
		tier = duchy
		name = gzw_title_gong_0139
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_landless_title = yes
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_wangjue value = yes }
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		set_coa = gzw_coa_0139
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzw_gong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wj_gong_0139
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_wjholder
		# 同姓/异姓: 与授封君主(最高领主)同王朝为同姓
		if = {
			limit = { dynasty = scope:guanzhi_liege.dynasty }
			add_trait = gzw_tong_gong
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = gzw_yi_gong
		}
		# 无地者: 王公爵头衔升主头衔(独立存在); 有地者主头衔不动
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI wangjue granted"
}
